## Exact Input and Consumer Contracts

```json
{
  "root": "🧰️framework/🛍️products/📓️print/📦️packages/🟦️typescript",
  "namedInputs": {
    "default": [
      "{projectRoot}/**/*",
      {
        "runtime": "node -p \"process.platform.concat(process.arch)\""
      },
      "{workspaceRoot}/🧰️framework/🛍️products/📓️print/**/*.{ts,tsx,js,jsx,mjs,cjs,json,css,scss,html,svg,wit,sql}",
      "{workspaceRoot}/package.json",
      "{workspaceRoot}/bun.lock",
      "{workspaceRoot}/bunfig.toml",
      "{workspaceRoot}/tsconfig.json",
      {
        "env": "NODE_ENV"
      },
      {
        "env": "BUN_ENV"
      },
      {
        "env": "CI"
      },
      {
        "env": "SEMIO_BUILD_MODE"
      },
      {
        "env": "SEMIO_RENDERER"
      },
      {
```
